# Commands · M · part 4

1,436 entries, memory-sweep to migrate. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **memory-sweep**: Maintenance pass over the project memory: archive resolved/stale worklog, keep the loaded index lean and durable, and regenerate it by value. This is the manual generator the memory architecture defers to (a cloud `/schedule` agent can't reach local `~/.claude` files). ([juan-guez/team-memory-plugin](https://github.com/juan-guez/team-memory-plugin/blob/HEAD/commands/memory-sweep.md))
